# Seed 44 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - small, the first: fsys 5 found its place at the small end (Iteration 230)

`seed 44 tag2 6h then experiment compare-fronts.py --rounds 6 --frontier
--cpus 2,8`, commit 3306c126 (Iteration 216: the after-seed-43 batch - fsys 4
buffered for real, fsys 5, READ-LINE tightened, the dump guard), started 13:08;
the evolution 13:10-17:16 (4 h 05 min, generations 0-89), the front measured
again by 17:30, the comparison packed by 19:07. 22,785 designs made, 18,094
measured (17,988 alive), 4,691 screened - not measured.

The comparison's own verdict (five measures): fastest -0.6%, smallest +0.00%,
memory +0 KB, hypervolume +0.9%, best Q -0.2% - **small: the first small run
since seed 43** (GOALS.md's rule: two small runs in a row, stop - new genes
before more runs). The Q-best design of all runs is seed 43's 0af26c8e89: 0.206
at 25,779 bytes, 180 KB, Q 0.211 of s6's (fc09f6373d, last session's, 0.205 at
25,964). The smallest of all runs is still seed 43's add5ec8937, 16,136 bytes
(seed 44 matched it: dde890d478, 16,136, speed 1.339); the fastest still seed
42's 28677d13b1 (0.181 at 31,608). Calibration s6/s6 1.002; cpu 2 against cpu 8
median 1.004, Spearman 1.00.

From the run's database (Iteration 230):

- Deaths: 106, every one "reach limit at scale 0 - not run"; none on the corpus.
- The screen: 4,691 of the 21,100 children from generation 5 skipped (22.2%);
  518 measured anyway (audited), none of them on the final front.
- Children by the try that made them: mutation 56.6%, crossover 32.2%,
  borrowed 11.2% (the last fifth of generations: crossover 35.5%) at XOVER 0.25
  - the remade-repeats share, as in seeds 42 and 43.
- Via the attic: 3,364 children; 7 of the 120 on the final front.
- The final front, 120 designs: mutation 82, borrowed 20, crossover 8, carried
  10 - 110 made in this run.
- fsys: 2 on 50, 3 on 29, 0 on 22, **5 on 15**, 4 on 3, 1 on 1. The smallest
  tenth (16,136-16,408 bytes) is all fsys 3; the small third (16,136-18,152)
  fsys 3 on 29, 5 on 8, 4 on 3; the fast third fsys 2 on 32, 0 on 8. fsys 5's
  15 run from 17,769 bytes up - the reading ahead pays where a design is no
  longer the very smallest.
- threads: 16 chains (threads=3) on 85 of the 120, the dump's 32 on 31.
- fbuf (designs with native file words): 1 KB buffers on 49, 4 KB on 23, 512
  bytes on 1 - Iteration 225's slots-apart patch makes 4 KB as cheap in memory.
- 5,580 designs made an hour (4,430 measured); seed 43 5,667.

---

# Evolved VM designs

18094 designs evaluated, 17988 alive, 4691 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): fc97d8fcf7, speed 0.184, binary + image 26,236, memory 180 KB - Q 0.193 of s6-cv8b's.
